// Generated by artie generate from github-reasoned.trace.yaml, the run of github-task-reasoned.kts.
// Every selection as that run made it, nested as it nested, with no model.
// What it was offered is pinned in github-task-from-reasoned.pins.yaml, held to before every act.
//   artie run github-task-from-reasoned.kts owner=JogoShugh repo=heart-github-test
val owner by input()
val repo by input()
val stamp by input(default = java.time.LocalDateTime.now().format(java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("MMdd-HHmmss")))

heart("http://localhost:8392/") {
    var issue = 0L // step 3's /number
    var pull = 0L // step 9's /number

    act("github:repos-get", "owner" to owner, "repo" to repo) {
        act(
            "github:open-issue",
            "title" to "Crash when a bed has no name (reasoned)",
            "body" to "Opened by a HEART script.",
        ) {
            issue = state.long("number")
        }
    }
    act("github:repos-get-branch", "owner" to owner, "repo" to repo, "branch" to "main") {
        act("github:branch-from-here", "name" to "fix-blank-names-reasoned-$stamp") {
            act(
                "github:commit-file",
                "path" to "fix-reasoned-$stamp.md",
                "message" to "Refuse a bed with no name",
                "content" to "Bed names may not be blank.",
            )
            act(
                "github:open-pull-request",
                "base" to "main",
                "title" to "Refuse a bed with no name (reasoned)",
                "body" to "Relates to #$issue",
            ) {
                pull = state.long("number")
                until(tries = 20) { offers("github:merge") }
                act("github:merge", "merge_method" to "squash")
                check("merged" eq true)
            }
        }
    }
    act("github:issues-get", "owner" to owner, "repo" to repo, "issue_number" to issue) {
        act("github:comment", "body" to "Fixed in 1.2 by #$pull.") {
            check("body" eq "Fixed in 1.2 by #$pull.")
        }
        act("github:close")
        check("state" eq "closed", "state_reason" eq "completed")
    }
}
